{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_7","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc 'cat /home/travis/.codex/skills/maven-build-lifecycle/SKILL.md'","aggregated_output":"---\nname: maven-build-lifecycle\ndescription: Use when working with Maven build phases, goals, profiles, or customizing the build process for Java projects.\n---\n\n# Maven Build Lifecycle\n\nMaster Maven's build lifecycle including phases, goals, profiles, and build customization for efficient Java project builds.\n\n## Overview\n\nMaven's build lifecycle is a well-defined sequence of phases that execute in order. Understanding the lifecycle is essential for effective build configuration and optimization.\n\n## Default Lifecycle Phases\n\n### Complete Phase Order\n\n```\n1.  validate      - Validate project structure\n2.  initialize    - Initialize build state\n3.  generate-sources\n4.  process-sources\n5.  generate-resources\n6.  process-resources - Copy resources to output\n7.  compile       - Compile source code\n8.  process-classes\n9.  generate-test-sources\n10. process-test-sources\n11. generate-test-resources\n12. process-test-resources\n13. test-compile  - Compile test sources\n14. process-test-classes\n15. test          - Run unit tests\n16. prepare-package\n17. package       - Create JAR/WAR\n18. pre-integration-test\n19. integration-test - Run integration tests\n20. post-integration-test\n21. verify        - Run verification checks\n22. install       - Install to local repo\n23. deploy        - Deploy to remote repo\n```\n\n### Common Phase Commands\n\n```bash\n# Compile only\nmvn compile\n\n# Compile and run tests\nmvn test\n\n# Create package\nmvn package\n\n# Install to local repository\nmvn install\n\n# Deploy to remote repository\nmvn deploy\n\n# Clean and build\nmvn clean install\n\n# Skip tests\nmvn install -DskipTests\n\n# Skip test compilation and execution\nmvn install -Dmaven.test.skip=true\n```\n\n## Clean Lifecycle\n\n```\n1. pre-clean\n2. clean         - Delete target directory\n3. post-clean\n```\n\n```bash\n# Clean build artifacts\nmvn clean\n\n# Clean specific directory\nmvn clean -DbuildDirectory=out\n```\n\n## Site Lifecycle\n\n```\n1. pre-site\n2. site          - Generate documentation\n3. post-site\n4. site-deploy   - Deploy documentation\n```\n\n```bash\n# Generate site\nmvn site\n\n# Generate and deploy site\nmvn site-deploy\n```\n\n## Goals vs Phases\n\n### Executing Phases\n\n```bash\n# Execute phase (runs all previous phases)\nmvn package\n```\n\n### Executing Goals\n\n```bash\n# Execute specific goal\nmvn compiler:compile\nmvn surefire:test\nmvn jar:jar\n\n# Multiple goals\nmvn dependency:tree compiler:compile\n```\n\n### Phase-to-Goal Bindings\n\n```xml\n<build>\n    <plugins>\n        <plugin>\n            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n            <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>\n            <version>3.12.1</version>\n            <executions>\n                <execution>\n                    <id>compile-sources</id>\n                    <phase>compile</phase>\n                    <goals>\n                        <goal>compile</goal>\n                    </goals>\n                </execution>\n            </executions>\n        </plugin>\n    </plugins>\n</build>\n```\n\n## Build Profiles\n\n### Profile Definition\n\n```xml\n<profiles>\n    <profile>\n        <id>development</id>\n        <activation>\n            <activeByDefault>true</activeByDefault>\n        </activation>\n        <properties>\n            <env>dev</env>\n            <skip.integration.tests>true</skip.integration.tests>\n        </properties>\n    </profile>\n\n    <profile>\n        <id>production</id>\n        <properties>\n            <env>prod</env>\n            <skip.integration.tests>false</skip.integration.tests>\n        </properties>\n        <build>\n            <plugins>\n                <plugin>\n                    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n                    <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>\n                    <configuration>\n                        <debug>false</debug>\n                        <optimize>true</optimize>\n                    </configuration>\n                </plugin>\n            </plugins>\n        </build>\n    </profile>\n</profiles>\n```\n\n### Profile Activation\n\n```bash\n# Activate by name\nmvn install -Pproduction\n\n# Multiple profiles\nmvn install -Pproduction,ci\n\n# Deactivate profile\nmvn install -P!development\n```\n\n### Activation Triggers\n\n```xml\n<profile>\n    <id>jdk17</id>\n    <activation>\n        <!-- Activate by JDK version -->\n        <jdk>17</jdk>\n    </activation>\n</profile>\n\n<profile>\n    <id>windows</id>\n    <activation>\n        <!-- Activate by OS -->\n        <os>\n            <family>windows</family>\n        </os>\n    </activation>\n</profile>\n\n<profile>\n    <id>ci</id>\n    <activation>\n        <!-- Activate by environment variable -->\n        <property>\n            <name>env.CI</name>\n            <value>true</value>\n        </property>\n    </activation>\n</profile>\n\n<profile>\n    <id>with-config</id>\n    <activation>\n        <!-- Activate by file existence -->\n        <file>\n            <exists>src/main/config/app.properties</exists>\n        </file>\n    </activation>\n</profile>\n```\n\n## Resource Filtering\n\n### Enable Filtering\n\n```xml\n<build>\n    <resources>\n        <resource>\n            <directory>src/main/resources</directory>\n            <filtering>true</filtering>\n            <includes>\n                <include>**/*.properties</include>\n                <include>**/*.xml</include>\n            </includes>\n        </resource>\n        <resource>\n            <directory>src/main/resources</directory>\n            <filtering>false</filtering>\n            <excludes>\n                <exclude>**/*.properties</exclude>\n                <exclude>**/*.xml</exclude>\n            </excludes>\n        </resource>\n    </resources>\n</build>\n```\n\n### Property Substitution\n\n```properties\n# application.properties\napp.name=${project.name}\napp.version=${project.version}\napp.environment=${env}\nbuild.timestamp=${maven.build.timestamp}\n```\n\n## Build Customization\n\n### Source and Target Configuration\n\n```xml\n<properties>\n    <maven.compiler.source>17</maven.compiler.source>\n    <maven.compiler.target>17</maven.compiler.target>\n    <maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>\n    <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>\n</properties>\n```\n\n### Custom Source Directories\n\n```xml\n<build>\n    <sourceDirectory>src/main/java</sourceDirectory>\n    <testSourceDirectory>src/test/java</testSourceDirectory>\n    <resources>\n        <resource>\n            <directory>src/main/resources</directory>\n        </resource>\n    </resources>\n    <testResources>\n        <testResource>\n            <directory>src/test/resources</directory>\n        </testResource>\n    </testResources>\n</build>\n```\n\n### Final Name and Output\n\n```xml\n<build>\n    <finalName>${project.artifactId}-${project.version}</finalName>\n    <directory>target</directory>\n    <outputDirectory>target/classes</outputDirectory>\n    <testOutputDirectory>target/test-classes</testOutputDirectory>\n</build>\n```\n\n## Multi-Module Builds\n\n### Reactor Options\n\n```bash\n# Build all modules\nmvn install\n\n# Build specific module and dependencies\nmvn install -pl module-name -am\n\n# Build dependents of a module\nmvn install -pl module-name -amd\n\n# Resume from specific module\nmvn install -rf :module-name\n\n# Build in parallel\nmvn install -T 4\nmvn install -T 1C  # 1 thread per CPU core\n```\n\n### Module Order Control\n\n```xml\n<!-- parent/pom.xml -->\n<modules>\n    <module>common</module>\n    <module>api</module>\n    <module>service</module>\n    <module>web</module>\n</modules>\n```\n\n## Test Configuration\n\n### Surefire Plugin (Unit Tests)\n\n```xml\n<plugin>\n    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n    <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>\n    <version>3.2.3</version>\n    <configuration>\n        <includes>\n            <include>**/*Test.java</include>\n            <include>**/*Tests.java</include>\n        </includes>\n        <excludes>\n            <exclude>**/*IntegrationTest.java</exclude>\n        </excludes>\n        <parallel>methods</parallel>\n        <threadCount>4</threadCount>\n        <forkCount>1</forkCount>\n        <reuseForks>true</reuseForks>\n    </configuration>\n</plugin>\n```\n\n### Failsafe Plugin (Integration Tests)\n\n```xml\n<plugin>\n    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n    <artifactId>maven-failsafe-plugin</artifactId>\n    <version>3.2.3</version>\n    <executions>\n        <execution>\n            <goals>\n                <goal>integration-test</goal>\n                <goal>verify</goal>\n            </goals>\n        </execution>\n    </executions>\n    <configuration>\n        <includes>\n            <include>**/*IT.java</include>\n            <include>**/*IntegrationTest.java</include>\n        </includes>\n    </configuration>\n</plugin>\n```\n\n## Build Optimization\n\n### Incremental Builds\n\n```bash\n# Skip unchanged modules\nmvn install -amd\n\n# Use build cache (requires Maven Daemon)\nmvnd install\n```\n\n### Parallel Builds\n\n```xml\n<build>\n    <plugins>\n        <plugin>\n            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n            <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>\n            <configuration>\n                <fork>true</fork>\n                <compilerArgs>\n                    <arg>-J-Xmx512m</arg>\n                </compilerArgs>\n            </configuration>\n        </plugin>\n    </plugins>\n</build>\n```\n\n### Build Cache\n\n```bash\n# Enable build cache (Maven 4+)\nmvn install -Dmaven.build.cache.enabled=true\n```\n\n## Debugging Builds\n\n### Verbose Output\n\n```bash\n# Debug mode\nmvn install -X\n\n# Error stacktrace\nmvn install -e\n\n# Quiet mode\nmvn install -q\n```\n\n### Effective POM\n\n```bash\n# View resolved POM\nmvn help:effective-pom\n\n# View effective settings\nmvn help:effective-settings\n\n# Active profiles\nmvn help:active-profiles\n```\n\n### Dependency Analysis\n\n```bash\n# Check plugin versions\nmvn versions:display-plugin-updates\n\n# Check dependency versions\nmvn versions:display-dependency-updates\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Use Clean Builds** - Run `mvn clean` before releases\n2. **Consistent Versions** - Lock plugin versions\n3. **Profile Isolation** - Keep profiles focused\n4. **Fail Fast** - Use `-ff` in CI for quick feedback\n5. **Parallel Builds** - Use `-T` for multi-module projects\n6. **Skip Wisely** - Know the difference between skip options\n7. **Resource Filtering** - Enable only where needed\n8. **Test Separation** - Unit tests in Surefire, integration in Failsafe\n9. **Reproducible Builds** - Pin all plugin versions\n10. **Document Profiles** - Comment profile purposes\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n1. **Skipping Tests** - Don't skip tests in CI\n2. **SNAPSHOT in Release** - Remove snapshots before release\n3. **Missing Clean** - Stale files causing issues\n4. **Profile Conflicts** - Overlapping profile configurations\n5. **Resource Filtering** - Accidentally filtering binaries\n6. **Phase Confusion** - Running wrong phase\n7. **Memory Issues** - Insufficient heap for large builds\n8. **Reactor Order** - Module dependency issues\n\n## CI/CD Integration\n\n### GitHub Actions\n\n```yaml\n- name: Build with Maven\n  run: mvn -B clean verify -Pci\n\n- name: Release\n  run: mvn -B deploy -Prelease -DskipTests\n```\n\n### Jenkins Pipeline\n\n```groovy\nstage('Build') {\n    steps {\n        sh 'mvn -B clean package -DskipTests'\n    }\n}\nstage('Test') {\n    steps {\n        sh 'mvn -B test'\n    }\n}\nstage('Integration Test') {\n    steps {\n        sh 'mvn -B verify -DskipUnitTests'\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## When to Use This Skill\n\n- Setting up new Maven projects\n- Customizing build phases\n- Creating build profiles for environments\n- Configuring test execution\n- Optimizing build performance\n- Debugging build failures\n- Setting up CI/CD pipelines\n- Multi-module project configuration\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

While package elements don't technically\n   * have a visibility associated with them, this method returns {@link #PUBLIC} for\n   * {@link PackageElement} instances.\n   */\n  public static  Visibility ofElement(Element element) {\n    checkNotNull(element);\n    // packages don't have modifiers, but they're obviously \"public\"\n    if (element.getKind().equals(PACKAGE)) {\n      return PUBLIC;\n    }\n    Set<Modifier> modifiers = element.getModifiers();\n    if (modifiers.contains(Modifier.PRIVATE)) {\n      return PRIVATE;\n    } else if (modifiers.contains(Modifier.PROTECTED)) {\n      return PROTECTED;\n    } else if (modifiers.contains(Modifier.PUBLIC)) {\n      return PUBLIC;\n    } else {\n      return DEFAULT;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Returns effective visibility of the given element meaning that it takes into account the\n   * visibility of its enclosing elements.\n   */\n  public static Visibility effectiveVisibilityOfElement(Element element) {\n    checkNotNull(element);\n    Visibility effectiveVisibility = PUBLIC;\n    Element currentElement = element;\n    while (currentElement != null) {\n      effectiveVisibility =\n          Ordering.natural().min(effectiveVisibility, ofElement(currentElement));\n      currentElement = currentElement.getEnclosingElement();\n    }\n    return effectiveVisibility;\n  }\n}","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

parameters;\n * <li>there is a public static method {@code ImmutableFoo.builder()} that returns\n *     {@code ImmutableFoo.Builder};\n * <li>there is a method {@code ImmutableFoo.Builder.build()} void testImmutableBuilders() throws Exception {\n    ClassPath classPath = ClassPath.from(getClass().getClassLoader());\n    ImmutableSet<ClassPath.ClassInfo> classes = classPath.getAllClasses();\n checkImmutableClass(Class<?> c)\n      throws ClassNotFoundException, NoSuchMethodException {\n    if (!Modifier.isPublic(c.getModifiers())) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // We have a public static ImmutableFoo.builder()\n    Method builderMethod = c.getMethod(\"builder\");\n    assertThat(Modifier.isStatic(builderMethod.getModifiers())).isTrue();\n\n    // Its return type is Builder with the expect.that(builderMethod.getReturnType()).isEqualTo(builderClass);\n    expect.that(Arrays.toString(builderMethodParameterizedReturn.getActualTypeArguments()))\n        .named(c.getName())\n        .isEqualTo(Arrays.toString(builderClass.getTypeParameters()));\n\n    // The Builder has a public build() method that returns ImmutableFoo.\n    Method buildMethod = builderClass.getMethod(\"build\");\n    expect.that(buildMethod.getReturnType()).isEqualTo(c);\n\n    // The Builder has either an addAll or a putAll public method with a parameter that\n    // && m.getParameterTypes().length == 1) {\n        Class<?> parameter = m.getParameterTypes()[0];\n        if (parameter.isAssignableFrom(c)) {\n          found = true;\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    expect.that(found).named(builderClass.getName() + \" has addAll or putAll\").isTrue();\n  }\n}\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}
